# Decision Log — operator decisions since the 2026-09-16 roadmap review

**How to use this file.** (1) This is the single index of operator decisions from the
2026-09-16 roadmap review onward — `engineering_roadmap.html` (v27) has not been updated since
2026-09-20 and no longer carries them. (2) A decision made from here on gets the next `D-NNN`
number and is added to this file **in the same PR** as its S1 doc's own `## Decision` section —
do not let the two drift apart again (this is exactly what happened to E-033 S1's 2026-09-22
"readers must see ALL variants" decision, lost between slices until the 2026-09-27 delivery
review re-found it). (3) The roadmap card a decision changes/confirms should cite the D-number
in its own text going forward. (4) Numbering is chronological by decision date, not by when it
was entered here. (5) A superseded decision is marked `superseded by D-xxx` and never deleted —
the sequence of correction is itself the record.

Status legend: **built** (shipped in code, flag may still be off) · **built-flag-off** (shipped,
gated behind an off-by-default flag) · **not built** (decision recorded, no code yet) ·
**superseded by D-xxx**.

---

| D-042 | 2026-09-28 | **"Different coin" variant may run on partial coverage.** It runs on the windows its coin covers, provided it covers at least ~60% of the windows ~~and at least 2 eras~~ (2-era condition superseded by D-045); time-dependent bars are normalised (trade minimum as a per-year rate plus an absolute floor; drawdown limit scaled to the period length so a shorter period cannot pass more easily); below the minimum coverage the variant is INCONCLUSIVE instead of blocking the whole idea. Resolves the parked G13 (the full-coverage default is withdrawn). | cards C, D, B | operator, 2026-09-28 session (challenge: full coverage would stop BTC being tested wherever another coin lacks data; metrics should be scale-standardised) | C2 part: `tools/variant_coin.py` (`D042_MIN_WINDOW_COVERAGE = 0.60`, Layer-1 precheck; the 2-era condition dropped by operator 2026-09-28 (D-045) -- the eras covered stay recorded in `coverage.eras`, information only) called by 5a in `run_phase1_research.py`; a coin below the floor is not_tested `insufficient_coverage:` and the grid lists it in `untested_variants`; so is an asset variant the Layer-2 data gate declines / refines (`insufficient_coverage: layer2`). A window counts only when the coin's Layer-1 earliest date is on or before its test start. Bars normalisation: not built -- until E-062 S2b a partial-coverage variant is graded but caps the grid at inconclusive and its time-dependent bars read NOT_EVALUABLE (never `profit_bars_reached`) | C2 part built-flag-off (E-061 C2 S2b, `orchestrator.variant_loop`); bars normalisation pending E-062 S2b |
| D-045 | 2026-09-28 | **Partial-coverage asset variant: drop the "at least 2 eras" condition; keep only ≥60% of the windows.** The policy's eras are cut by data-feed availability (pre-funding vs full feed), not by market phases, so on a 2018–2023 test any coin starting after 2019-09 was refused even at ~70% coverage. Robustness across eras stays covered by the `sign_consistent_by_era` criterion. | cards C, D | operator, 2026-09-28 session ("Ok for the drop on era"), on the orchestrator's recommendation | `tools/variant_coin.py` (`D042_MIN_ERAS` removed; eras recorded as information only, midpoint rule) | built-flag-off (E-061 C2 S2b) |

| D-138 | 2026-10-15 | **Step 1a's extra hypothesis cards are enqueued as soon as step 1a is recorded completed, whatever fails later in the run, behind `orchestrator.extra_cards_at_1a` (off by default; off = byte-identical).** Branch independence, PR 1 of 5. **Found on run_094 (2026-10-14):** 1a completed and wrote one extra card, protocol_execution then raised (CompositeError), run_loop's `except` set the run `failed`, and the only enqueue point, `_enqueue_queued_hypotheses` (`workflow/run_campaign.py::_enqueue_queued_hypotheses`), called after run_loop returns (`_record_run_loop_children`), refuses any run whose status is `failed` ("NOT enqueued -- step 1a did not complete cleanly (status='failed')"); the card reached the queue only when the operator resumed the run and it completed. **The marker of "1a completed":** `hypothesis_generation` in `pipeline_state.yaml` `completed_stages`, written by run_loop's "6. Mark completed" step only after every 1a check (the split, the repeat check, the pass-rule write) passed -- any of them raising goes to `except` first. **Flag on:** run_loop takes an optional `after_1a` callable (`workflow/run_phase1_research.py::run_loop`), called right after that step for `hypothesis_generation`; process_once passes `_extra_cards_at_1a_hook` (`workflow/run_campaign.py::_extra_cards_at_1a_hook`), which runs the same `_enqueue_queued_hypotheses` with `whatever_fails_later=True` (1a completed is then the only condition). The file is then marked `enqueued`, so the enqueue after run_loop is a no-op (one queue entry per card, ever; it also no longer refuses a run that failed after 1a, as a fallback); the queue is re-read before process_once saves it. A hook failure is logged and does not fail the run; the enqueue after run_loop tries again and raises as before. A 1a that did not complete enqueues nothing (unchanged). No new guard; branches 2 and 3, decide-next and regroup/record are untouched (later PRs). | card M (card H, branch independence) | operator ask and advisory 2026-10-15 ("queue step 1a's extra cards once 1a completes, whatever fails later"); direction ruling (B) | `workflow/run_phase1_research.py::_extra_cards_at_1a_enabled`, `::run_loop`; `workflow/run_campaign.py::_extra_cards_at_1a_hook`, `::_record_run_loop_children`, `::_run_after_preflight`, `::process_once`; `tests/test_d138_extra_cards_at_1a.py` | built-flag-off (`orchestrator.extra_cards_at_1a`) |
| D-139 | 2026-10-15 | **A pre-registered claim is not re-authored by a model: (a) code keeps a decide-next brief's pre-filled claim in step 1a's card when it validates; (b) step 1a receives `CLAIM_TESTS_TRADE.md` under `orchestrator.analyst.enabled`.** **The ruling, verbatim (direction, operator-relayed):** "Q1 trade-test gap = a FIX, in scope (no new lens, tool or claim kind; an approved output of the analyst was dropped by step 1a). One D-row; one PR each unless you judge them one change; flag off = byte-identical; no new flag, no new gate." One PR, two commits (same defect). **Evidence, run_095 (operator's checkout, flags analyst / folds / claim_tests / decide_next on):** `research_brief.yaml` `candidate.claim` carried two valid trade-level tests (selector `trade` where `entry_forecast >= 10` / `<= -10`, outcome `trade_net_return`, baseline `other_trades`, `mean_diff`, `less`; kind `execution_behaviour`); step 1a read only `CLAIM_TESTS.md` (no trade tests) and wrote `tests: none`, kind `conditional_behaviour`, missing_block "No outcome, selector or baseline slot in CLAIM_TESTS.md expresses it"; `claim_test_status.yaml` read `tests_none`, `claim_prefill.yaml` recorded `changed: [kind, tests (spec_hash)]`, the claim revision after 1b was frozen on 1a's version (D-100), and the child's claim was never measured (fold confirmation `not_comparable`). **(a) What:** `_keep_prefilled_claim` (`workflow/run_phase1_research.py:17879`), called first inside `_check_claim_after_1a`'s try (`:18285`), before `_claim_check_cards` (`:18289`), the claim-test status / `tests: none` request row / coverage (`_finish_claim_status`, `:18323`) and everything after 1a that reads the card's claim (pass-rule write `:20590`, the gate before 1b, claim revision, claim match, claim measurement); again after a claim retry that wrote cards (`:18304`; restored with the first answer when the retry is undone, `:18313`). It uses decide-next's own test of the claim: `claim_card.check_claim(pre, **_claim_check_kw())`, the call `reader_findings.side_finding_review` makes (folds kinds and, under the analyst flag, the trade family), valid = no errors and not `tests: none` (decide-next's eligibility, `tools/decide_next.py:1640-1645`). Valid: the whole claim (statement, kind, tests, pass_if, fail_if, rationale) is written into `hypothesis_card.yaml` unchanged (nothing else in the card changes; an exact copy is not rewritten), as D-110 writes the brief's config: code writes, the model's version is not used for that part, the record is kept. `_check_prefilled_claim` (`:17909`, called at `:18326`, unchanged position) gains `own_claim`: the claim 1a wrote, captured before the overwrite, so `claim_prefill.yaml` and its warning are exactly what they were, the record of 1a's own difference. No pre-filled claim, an invalid one, no card, or `claim_tests` off: nothing changes (byte-identical card). Consequence: a broken or `tests: none` 1a claim on such a run no longer costs a claim retry (the kept claim is the one checked). `PREFILLED_CLAIM.md`: the escape clause ("If the claim cannot be kept as it is ... the run continues either way.") is removed; the first bullet adds "Code keeps this claim in your card after this step, as it is, and records any difference in yours." **(b) What:** `_apply_claim_tests_context` (`:17964`) appends `CLAIM_TESTS_TRADE_GUIDE` (`:17861`) to `hypothesis_generation`'s inputs right after `CLAIM_TESTS.md` when `_analyst_enabled()` (`:17991-17996`), the pattern of `CLAIM_TESTS.md` / `PREFILLED_CLAIM.md` / `NEAREST_BUILD.md` (a skill file appended to a stage's inputs under its flag, inside the function's existing `claim_tests` gate). `CLAIM_TESTS_TRADE.md`'s header now says it is shown only under that flag, to the analyst's prompt and step 1a's. **DECLARED PROMPT CHANGE (under `orchestrator.analyst.enabled` only; on in the operator's checkout):** step 1a's inputs gain `CLAIM_TESTS_TRADE.md` on every run with the analyst on; the `PREFILLED_CLAIM.md` text changes for every pre-filled-claim brief (reached only under `claim_tests`). Analyst off: step 1a's inputs byte-identical. No pinned skill file changed (`hypothesis-design/SKILL.md` untouched). **Gating:** (a) the existing `claim_tests` gate of `_check_claim_after_1a` plus the input (a brief with `candidate.claim`, written by decide-next under `decide_next`); the trade family validates only under `analyst` (`_claim_check_kw`). (b) `analyst`. No new flag, gate, check or warning (one info line `[D-139]` when the card is overwritten). **Tests:** `tests/test_d139_prefilled_claim_kept.py` (21: claim_tests off / no pre-filled claim / invalid pre-filled claim leave the card byte-identical; a valid one replaces 1a's `tests: none`, the record still lists 1a's difference, no request row; a broken 1a claim needs no retry; an exact copy is not rewritten; after a retry the retry's card is kept and recorded, after an undone retry the first answer's record returns; run_095's claim kept with the analyst on, today's `tests_none` with it off; 1a's inputs gain the trade guide iff the analyst is on, byte-identical off; mutations 7 of 7 killed); `tests/test_d091_trade_tests_pipeline.py` check_claim call count 10 to 11; `tests/test_e075_pr3_trade_tests.py` guard now allows `run_phase1_research.py` to name the trade guide. **Number:** D-138 is the highest on master; no open PR at writing. | E-068 slice 5; E-075 (analyst); D-073, D-091, D-100 | direction ruling 2026-10-15, operator-relayed; precedent D-110 | `workflow/run_phase1_research.py` (`_keep_prefilled_claim`, `_check_prefilled_claim`, `_check_claim_after_1a`, `_apply_claim_tests_context`); `workflow_artifacts/skills/hypothesis-design/PREFILLED_CLAIM.md`, `CLAIM_TESTS_TRADE.md`; `tests/test_d139_prefilled_claim_kept.py` | built (no new flag; declared prompt change under `orchestrator.analyst.enabled`) |
| D-140 | 2026-10-15 | **A cache merge that adds no rows is not written back (`BaseFetcher._merge_and_store`).** **Found on run_096 (fold C, 2018-01..2021-08, kraken perp; twice):** protocol_execution's grid evaluation raised `CompositeError`; the composite subprocess died in `data_manager._premerge_aux_feeds` -> `base_fetcher._load_all` -> `_merge_and_store` `combined.to_csv(path)` with `PermissionError [Errno 13]` on `local_data/fear_greed_daily.csv`. **Defect (verified):** `_load_all` re-requests every missing period on every read, including ones the source can never fill (fear_greed starts 2018-02-01 and has a 2018-04-14..16 hole); the fetch returns no rows, `combined` equals the loaded cache, and `to_csv` re-opened the shared file for writing anyway. Every fold-C protocol subprocess rewrote it (first window; second window too for long warm-ups); run_095 (fold D) never reaches those dates, so it never wrote. **Holder (not identified):** no code path holds the file: protocol subprocesses are sequential (`composite_cache.py` `subprocess_runner`, `run_phase1_research.py` variant loop), the orchestrator never opens it, a Python read handle in another process does not block `to_csv` (measured), and a memory map gives `[Errno 22]`, not 13 (measured); the same 4-variant + composite sequence run from a worktree did not fail. Errno 13 matches a handle opened without write-sharing by something outside the code (antivirus or indexer most likely; Avast and McAfee are active on this machine). **Fix:** skip the write when `combined.equals(existing)`; the in-memory frame is assigned first, so the rows a run sees are unchanged, and a merge that adds, replaces or reorders rows still writes exactly as before. Not chosen: temp file + `os.replace` (measured: fails with WinError 5 whenever any other process has the file open, which is worse). **Not fixed:** a write that does add rows can still meet the same outside handle; the unfillable period is still re-requested from the network on every read. **No lookahead:** no row is added, removed or reordered in memory or on disk. **Tests:** `trading-bot/tests/test_merge_noop_no_rewrite.py` (4: no-op read leaves bytes and mtime unchanged; read succeeds while a handle denies write-sharing, which raised run_096's Errno 13 before the fix; added rows are still written; a normalising merge is still written). | D-147 | 2026-10-18 | **A component waits for its history ops' stated minimum before it forecasts. Unflagged engine fix (the D-142 route); nothing re-graded.** **Found:** a component's history fills only once the component is ready (`trading-bot/strategies/strategy_engine.py:413-425`, `update()`). Engine readiness was `len(history) >= _warmup`, with `_warmup = min(required_bars, smallest deque)` (constructor `:365`, `set_warmup` `:396-405`). The history ops' minimums (`trading-bot/strategies/registry.py:178` `TRANSFORM_MIN_PERIODS`, `:198` `transform_min_periods`; `ema` needs 3 x span) only sized the deque (`registry.py:267` `component_effective_lookback`, called from the engine's lookback phase at `strategy_engine.py:309`) and were absent from `get_required_periods`. `apply_transform_pipeline` (`registry.py:150`) has no short-history guard, and the forecast guard was `len(h) < 2`. So on the backtest and live path (`AdvancedStrategy`, which sets `_warmup` to `required_bars`, capped at the smallest deque) a history op's minimum never entered `required_bars`: run_071's `ratio_to_mean` components forecast once they held 24 values (the 24-bar floor), not 30; a standalone engine (tests only) forecast from 2. On that path the fix's effect is the higher `required_bars` of the nine configs below; `need` binds alone only where a smaller deque caps `_warmup` below a component's minimum. The gap policy's defaults follow the new `required_bars` too (`trading-bot/core/trading_bot.py:314` `large_min_bars`, `:396` the middle-tier entry block). **What changed:** `registry.HISTORY_OPS` (`registry.py:213`, one definition; `trading-bot/tools/validate_config.py:11` and `:127` (V5) import it) and `history_op_min_periods` (`registry.py:217-223`: largest history-op minimum over `transforms` + `history_transforms`, 1 when none). Per component the engine builds `_op_min` and `_need = max(2, _op_min)` in the constructor (`strategy_engine.py:334-345`; block regimes are in neither). `is_ready` requires `len(h) >= max(_warmup, need)` (`:558-569`); `forecast` returns 0.0 with `not_ready_component` below `need` (`:620-621`); `get_required_periods` adds, per component, its own periods + op minimum - 1 (`:571-578`; the floor of 2 is not added there, so a component with no history op keeps its periods, which keeps the 20 unchanged configs identical); the block chain is untouched. The warm-up prefetch (`trading-bot/core/launcher.py:950`, `2 * strategy.required_bars`) and its readiness assert (`:1022`) follow `required_bars`; so does the D-056 forecast-size probe (`strategy-research/tools/forecast_size_probe.py:204` and `:206`, `2 * strategy.required_bars`). **Measured (2026-10-18; constructing `AdvancedStrategy(config_path=...)` for each `strategy-research/runs/run_0NN/artifacts/candidate_strategy_config.json` from run_060 on, 29 files, constructor only, no backtest):** `required_bars` moves for nine configs only: run_064 50 to 79, run_070 24 to 31, run_073 24 to 32, run_074 24 to 26, run_075 24 to 31, run_071/076/077/078 24 to 49; the other 20 and `trading-bot/strategy_config.json` (`required_bars` 120) are unchanged. The strategy engine's own `get_required_periods` for `strategy_config.json` moves 15 to 44; `required_bars` stays 120 because a larger requirement sets it. **run_064 waits 79 history values though its op needs 30,** because `_warmup` tracks `required_bars` (CUL-273), capped at the smallest deque; accepted by the operator. **Re-runs:** the nine configs differ in the early bars of each window and converge once the deque cycles; records untouched (no run re-graded). The composite cache rebuilds on the new engine sha (`strategy-research/tools/composite_cache.py:358-362`: the cache directory name includes the candidate fingerprint's `git_sha`). **Tests:** `trading-bot/tests/test_history_op_readiness.py` (new, 31). Three existing comparison tests (`tests/test_e060_block_standardisation.py::test_engine_without_block_keys_matches_pre_s3a_engine_bar_for_bar`, `tests/test_vol_target_transform.py::test_engine_without_vol_target_matches_master_bar_for_bar` and `::test_shipped_strategy_config_required_bars_unchanged`) compare the engine against an old engine on legacy configs that use `ratio_to_mean` / `zscore` / `percentile`, so they fail by design; they now patch `history_op_min_periods` to 1 so they still compare only their own change (**OPEN:** the ruling said those byte-identity tests pass unchanged; `test_e060_block_standardisation.py` was one of them; operator to approve or reject that commit). | D-148 | 2026-10-18 | **`standardise`: a history op that puts a component on the forecast scale of its own past values. No flag; byte-identical for every config that does not use it.** **What it computes (`trading-bot/strategies/registry.py:137-146`, `_standardise`):** `clip(target * h[-1] / mean(|h| over the last window values before the current one), -cap, cap)`, where `h` is the component's stored history and `h[-1]` the current value (`past = h.iloc[-(window + 1):-1]`, `:141`). It is scale only: no mean is subtracted, so the sign and a zero survive. The arithmetic is `standardise_block_forecast` (`:304-311`), the function the block standardisation already uses, so the guard is the same: 0.0 when the past mean is not above 1e-10 (the `ratio_to_mean` guard). It also returns 0.0 while fewer than `min_periods` past values exist (`:142-143`); NaN entries in the past are skipped by the mean. **Params:** `window` (int >= 2) and `min_periods` (int, 2 <= `min_periods` <= `window`) are required; `target` defaults to 10 (the forecast scale's average absolute value) and `cap` to 20 (the engine's forecast cap), both finite numbers > 0 (`STANDARDISE_DEFAULTS`, `:112`). Bools, floats for the ints, zero, negatives, inf, NaN and non-dict params are refused by `standardise_param_errors` (`:115-134`); the op raises `ValueError` on exactly those params (`:138-140`). **Why this cannot leak:** only values before the current bar enter the mean; the current value is not in its own denominator, and every stored value was appended at or before this bar. A test pins that a huge current value is not damped by itself and that a value outside the window has no effect. **Engine sizing, two points flagged for the operator:** (1) **OPEN (orchestrator reading, operator to confirm):** the ruling said `transform_min_periods` returns `min_periods`; + 1 counts the current value, as the block standardisation counts `min_periods` past values (`trading-bot/strategies/strategy_engine.py:479`, `len(block.hist) >= reg["min_periods"]` before the current value is pushed), so the first forecast after the wait is not 0.0. `transform_min_periods("standardise", params)` returns `min_periods + 1` (`registry.py:259-264`; 2, the table entry at `:240`, for invalid params, which the op and V15 refuse). (2) **OPEN (orchestrator reading, operator to confirm):** the ruling did not size the deque; `window + 1` lets the op see its full window (the current value plus `window` past ones). `component_effective_lookback` holds `window + 1` for it (`registry.py:327-330`), which sets the deque size at `strategy_engine.py:309-310` and `:331`. A component whose own need or explicit `lookback` is larger keeps that. **Validator:** V5 now lists `standardise` as a history op (`trading-bot/tools/validate_config.py:14-15`), so it must come before any scalar or data-aware op. **V15** (`validate_config.py:370-378`) checks the params of every `standardise` step with `standardise_param_errors`, the same function the op uses, as V14 shares `vol_target_param_errors` with `vol_target`; so V15 fails on exactly the params the op raises on, and is silent for every config without the op. **Guide:** one row in the History ops table and one V15 row in `strategy-research/docs/STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md`; the V6 sentence names `standardise` `min_periods` + 1 (see the declared prompt change below). **V15 placement (operator ruling 2026-10-18):** `standardise` is the first step of `transforms` and the only history op in that list, because a history op reads the raw history and ignores the running value (`registry.py` `apply_transform_pipeline`); in `history_transforms` it is refused (it would see one value and always give 0). **Byte identity:** a config without `standardise` runs exactly as before: no existing op's code or min-periods entry changed, `component_effective_lookback` takes `window_need` = 1 when there is no such step, and `component_effective_lookback` is compared with the commit before this op (`022226b2`) on every legacy and `strategy_config.json` component spec (the old-engine bar-for-bar run imports the new registry, so it pins the engine, not the registry). **Depends on D-147 (#469), merge it first:** `get_required_periods` reads the history-op minimum only through D-147's `history_op_min_periods`; without it the engine does not wait for `min_periods` + 1 values (the op returns 0.0 until it has them, while the strategy may already trade). On rebase onto D-147, `standardise` moves into `registry.HISTORY_OPS`. An explicit `lookback` below `window` + 1 shortens the window silently (V6 checks `min_periods` + 1 only), as for the other ops. Composite cache: rebuilds on the new engine sha (`strategy-research/tools/composite_cache.py:358-362`). **Tests:** `trading-bot/tests/test_standardise_op.py` (58: hand-computed values, sign, both caps, custom target/cap, the 1e-10 guard, the `min_periods` wait (exactly `min_periods` past values is non-zero), current value not in its own denominator, only the last `window` past values count, NaN skipped, every param refusal and the op raising the same, V15 lines, V5 after a scalar op, `transform_min_periods` = `min_periods` + 1, deque `window` + 1, an engine run where the forecast equals the op on the stored history, byte identity); 58 passed. Neighbour set (that file + `test_validate_config.py`, `test_e060_block_standardisation.py`, `test_vol_target_transform.py`, `test_engine_warmth.py`, `test_validate_config_component_existence.py`): 174 passed. **Two existing op-set tests updated for the new key (the vol_target PR's precedent):** `tests/test_e060_block_standardisation.py::test_transform_ops_registry_is_unchanged` lists `standardise` with a comment; `tests/test_vol_target_transform.py::test_every_other_op_is_unchanged` expects the added set `{vol_target, standardise}` and excludes `standardise` from the `TRANSFORM_MIN_PERIODS` comparison. Nothing else in those files changed. **Declared prompt change (the config-writing stage reads both files):** `STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md` gets the `standardise` row, the V15 row, "Seventeen ops" and `standardise` in the minimum-periods paragraph; `COMPONENT_CATALOG.md` says "the only seventeen" and lists `standardise` among the history ops (`tests/test_design_guide_in_sync.py` checks both counts). `workflow/run_phase1_research.py` `_TRANSFORM_OP_NAMES` (O-21: a component_gap's `tried` must name a transform) gains `standardise` (`tests/test_o21_component_gap_tried.py` keeps it equal to the registry). strategy-research neighbours (`test_design_guide_in_sync.py`, `test_design_guide_examples.py`, `test_e073_data_dictionary.py`, `test_o21_component_gap_tried.py`, `test_e056_config_direct_authoring.py`): 165 passed. Not handled (as for `vol_target`): a `cap`/`target` int too large for a float makes the param check raise TypeError instead of returning an error; NaN past values count toward `min_periods` (the mean skips them, an all-NaN past gives 0.0). After the rebase onto D-147 (#469): `standardise` is in `registry.HISTORY_OPS` (so `history_op_min_periods` makes the engine wait for `min_periods` + 1 values), `test_history_op_readiness.py`'s membership test lists it, and the byte-identity tests compare with `e981f578` (master after #469). trading-bot 7 files 210 passed; strategy-research 4 files 117 passed. | none | operator GO 2026-10-18 (direction ruling, item 5 PR-B) | `trading-bot/strategies/registry.py` (`_standardise`, `standardise_param_errors`, `transform_min_periods`, `component_effective_lookback`); `trading-bot/tools/validate_config.py` (V5, V15); `strategy-research/docs/STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md`, `strategy-research/docs/COMPONENT_CATALOG.md`, `strategy-research/workflow/run_phase1_research.py` (`_TRANSFORM_OP_NAMES`); `trading-bot/tests/test_standardise_op.py`, two op-set tests | built (no flag) |
| D-149 | 2026-10-18 | **A forecast component's scale is standardised: its `transforms` holds `standardise` first and no `scale` op, or holds `vol_target`, or its regime is a blocks regime with `block_standardisation`; every other component is refused after 1b and at 5a (D-051 door). No flag; no existing run re-run; the check applies to new configs. Declared prompt change; code, docs and tests.** **Found (2026-10-18 direction ruling, "PR-C the rule, docs and check"):** the design guide advised "To size the forecast, put a `scale` op after the normalisation (for example `ratio_to_mean` then `scale` 10 gives an average absolute forecast of about 10)." (`docs/STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md`, the paragraph "`scaling_factor` cancels under normalisation"), so a config sized by a fixed multiplier on a ratio passed every check although its forecast size depends on the data. **The rule, strict (operator rulings 2026-10-18, decisions 6 and 7; `tools/forecast_rules.py`, `_not_standardised`, called from `strategies_violations`):** for each component of `strategies.regimes.<r>.components[j]`: PASS it when its regime dict has both `blocks` and `block_standardisation`; PASS it when its `transforms` holds a `vol_target` op (with or without a `scale`); PASS it when its `transforms` holds a `standardise` op and no `scale` op; REFUSE every other component. So these are refused: a bare `scale`, a `scaling_factor` param alone, `scale` only in `history_transforms`, `standardise` together with `scale` in one `transforms` list, `standardise` only in `history_transforms`, and a component with no scale op and no `scaling_factor` at all (its class default scale is not standardised; the earlier, looser rule let it through). A `scaling_factor` param next to a `standardise` is not refused (it is not a scale op, and the statistic divides it out). The regime detector is not checked. A non-dict component is skipped, as for D-051; a dict component whose `transforms` is not a list holds no ops and is refused. **That `standardise` is the FIRST step of `transforms`, and the only history op in its list, is `trading-bot/tools/validate_config.py` V15, not this check:** `forecast_rules` reads only the set of op names. Because the check is inside `strategies_violations`, both doors apply it with no call-site change: right after 1b (`workflow/run_phase1_research.py` `_base_forecast_rule_violations`, `fr.strategies_violations(config, fr.on_off_classes())`, one retry of 1b with the error, then `forecast_rule_violation`) and at 5a on every variant's config after its patch (`_fr.strategies_violations(variant_config, _fr_on_off)`). **The message:** `D-149: <loc> (id '<id>') -- ` followed by the module constant `RULE_SENTENCE`: "Standardised forecast (hard rule). Every component in strategies puts standardise first in its transforms, and no scale op after it (standardise: past values only, target 10, cap 20, window, min_periods), unless its scale is set by vol_target or the regime is a blocks regime with block_standardisation. Any other component, including one whose only scale is a bare scale or scaling_factor, is refused after 1b and again at 5a." **Three places, byte-for-byte the same sentence:** `forecast_rules.RULE_SENTENCE`; `docs/STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md` (it replaces the "To size the forecast ..." sentence quoted above, in the "`scaling_factor` cancels under normalisation" paragraph; the rest of the paragraph is kept); and `workflow_artifacts/skills/strategy-config-authoring/SKILL.md` (a new paragraph right after the "Graded components only (D-051, hard rule)" paragraph). One more sentence in the guide's "What the engine does with a spec": the bullet now reads "A forecast needs at least 2 history entries for every component of the regime, and a component with a history op waits for that op's stated minimum of values (Minimum periods, below) before its first forecast; else the forecast is 0.0." **Declared prompt change (decision 5 approved by the operator 2026-10-18):** the guide (the two sentences above), the skill paragraph, and one bullet in each of two skills, `strategy-config-authoring/SKILL.md` and `backtest-engineering/SKILL.md`, which replaced the bullet "scaling_factor and transform pipeline interact: if using ratio_to_mean + scale ... scaling_factor IS the forecast magnitude" (it contradicted the rule) with, word for word the same in both: "the forecast's scale (D-149): a component's transforms must hold standardise (first, no scale op after it) or vol_target, or its regime must be a blocks regime with block_standardisation; anything else is refused after 1b and again at 5a." The guide and the first skill are read by 1b. **Depends on:** PR #469 (D-147: a component waits for its history ops' minimum; the readiness sentence states that behaviour) and PR #470 (D-148: the `standardise` op the rule names, and V15). Both are on master; this branch is rebased on them. **Consequences (operator rulings 2026-10-18):** children of runs 071, 078, 079 and 094 to 096 are refused after 1b until a standardised host exists. The five queue entries `blocked_on_operator_approval` (`component_attribution-run_071-1`, `trade_efficiency-run_071-1`, `trade_efficiency-run_078-1`, `trade_efficiency-run_079-1`, `forecast_power-run_078-1`) stay blocked until the operator rules on them. PR-C option 1: merge this PR and re-base the host (a re-base brief follows in its own PR). **Existing configs under the strict rule (measured 2026-10-18, read-only: `strategies_violations` on each `runs/run_0NN/artifacts/candidate_strategy_config.json`, NN >= 60; 29 files exist):** 11 pass (runs 081 to 085 and 088 to 093); 18 are refused by D-149 (runs 060, 061, 063, 064, 065, 066, 070, 071, 073, 074, 075, 076, 077, 078, 079, 094, 095, 096; run 060 is also refused by D-051). No run was re-run. **Guide and skill examples refused** (found by running `strategies_violations` on every JSON block under the first, looser rule; none was changed; not re-measured under the strict rule, which can only refuse more): `docs/STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md` examples `two_trend_horizons`, `subtract_short_term_run_up`, `centre_one_sided_signal`, `sign_flip_negate`, `long_only_from_signed` and `normalised_trend`, and the unmarked single-component snippet (ratio_to_mean then scale 10, `scaling_factor` 0.4). They are executed by `tests/test_design_guide_examples.py`, which checks `validate_config` and the guide's stated property, not the D-149 rule, so it still passes. Decision 8 (bring the guide examples and the composition advice at "size the pipelines with `scale`" in line with the rule) is a separate follow-up docs PR, not this one. **Existing-test fixtures changed** (the rule is strict, so each fixture component is made standardised; the file's own subject is not scaling; `standardise` params `{"window": 20, "min_periods": 5}`): `tests/test_e061_end_to_end_wiring.py` `BASE_CONFIG` `rsi` `transforms[0]` `ratio_to_mean` -> `standardise` (`scaling_factor` 0.4 removed from the params and `scale` -> `identity` at `[1]` by the first PR-C commit; `[2]` `clip` and so `BAD_OP` `transforms/2` unchanged; `history_transforms` `vol_normalize` is a different list, so V15 holds); `tests/test_d051_d053_forecast_rules.py` `CONFIG` `transforms[0]` `identity` -> `standardise`, the dead-zone test builds `[standardise, <op>]` for `transforms`, the malformed-pieces test ignores D-149 messages for the dict component with a non-list `transforms`, and the `run_911` base `transforms[0]` `identity` -> `standardise`; `tests/test_e056_config_direct_authoring.py` `_BASE_CONFIG` and `tests/test_e056_1b_block_manifest.py` `CONFIG`: `transforms` `[identity]` -> `[standardise]`. **No lookahead:** a config check and prompt text; no data is read. **Tests:** `tests/test_d149_standardised_forecast_rule.py` (18 passed: `standardise` with `scale` (either order), a bare `scale`, `scaling_factor` alone, no scale at all, `scale` in `history_transforms`, `standardise` only in `history_transforms`, and `blocks` alone are refused; `standardise` alone, with clip, with negate, with a `scaling_factor` param, `vol_target` with and without `scale`, and a `blocks` + `block_standardisation` regime pass; the regime detector and malformed pieces are not checked; the message equals the location prefix + `RULE_SENTENCE`; the sentence appears once, byte-for-byte, in the guide and in the skill; the post-1b door sends a non-standardised base back to 1b with the sentence; the 5a door refuses a variant that adds a `scale` and one that drops `standardise`, and passes a negated and a `vol_target` variant). Run with the files above and the doors' neighbours (`tests/test_d051_d053_forecast_rules.py` 47 passed, `tests/test_e056_config_direct_authoring.py` 48, `tests/test_e056_1b_block_manifest.py` 99, `tests/test_design_guide_in_sync.py` 13, `tests/test_design_guide_examples.py` 29, `tests/test_d056_forecast_size_probe.py` 41, `tests/test_e061_end_to_end_wiring.py` 33 passed); 49 test files that use the doors or `config_direct_authoring` (they include `test_d056_forecast_size_probe`, `test_design_guide_in_sync` and `test_design_guide_examples` above) in one pytest process: 2278 passed. Follow-up (Decision 8, docs PR): the guide's six marked examples, the composition / sign-flip advice and the `vol_normalize` / `vol_adjusted` op rows now show the standardised pattern, and the production snippet gains a standardised twin (the snippet itself stays pinned to `trading-bot/strategy_config.json`, which D-149 would refuse as a new config); test_design_guide_examples.py checks every marked example passes D-149 (declared prompt change). | none | operator rulings, 2026-10-18 ("PR-C the rule, docs and check"; decisions 5 to 8); builds on D-051, D-147, D-148 | `strategy-research/tools/forecast_rules.py`; `strategy-research/docs/STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md`; `strategy-research/workflow_artifacts/skills/strategy-config-authoring/SKILL.md`; `strategy-research/workflow_artifacts/skills/backtest-engineering/SKILL.md`; `strategy-research/tests/test_d149_standardised_forecast_rule.py`; `strategy-research/tests/test_d051_d053_forecast_rules.py`; `strategy-research/tests/test_e056_config_direct_authoring.py`; `strategy-research/tests/test_e056_1b_block_manifest.py`; `strategy-research/tests/test_e061_end_to_end_wiring.py` (fixtures) | built (no flag; declared prompt change) |
| D-150 | 2026-10-18 | **Branch 3 records each variant's forecast shape (information only, never a bar, never a gate). No flag.** **Ruling (operator, 2026-10-18, item 7, verbatim):** "(7) Branch 3 information, own PR, D-row, no flag. _evaluate_profit_bars_every_backtest (workflow/run_phase1_research.py:16201 onward) adds a per-variant key forecast_shape beside bars (the per-variant dict {**entry, result, reason, bars, reasons}): abs_mean of forecast, share of bars with |forecast| at 20, abs_mean of postRebalance_current_allocation, n_bars, n_windows, read from the variant's window bars.csv files under runs/<run>/variants/<variant>/results/. Never inside bars or reasons, so the passing rule is untouched; information only, never a bar, never a gate. Check whether tools/campaign_memory.py:647's copy of the per-variant block carries the new key and say so in the PR; campaign_memory.schema.json is warn-only, extend it if the key would warn." **What it records (`workflow/run_phase1_research.py`):** `_variant_forecast_shape(run_dir, protocol_result)` (`:16208`) returns `{abs_mean_forecast, share_at_cap, abs_mean_allocation, n_bars, n_windows}`. `abs_mean_forecast` is the mean of |`forecast`| and `abs_mean_allocation` the mean of |`postRebalance_current_allocation`|, each over the finite values; `share_at_cap` is the share of finite forecasts with |f| >= 20 - 1e-9 (`_FORECAST_SHAPE_CAP`, `:16205`; the engine clips the ensemble to +-20, `trading-bot/strategies/strategy_engine.py:613`); `n_bars` is the number of rows read, `n_windows` the number of `bars.csv` files read; all numbers rounded to 8 decimals. **From where:** the graded windows only, i.e. the entries of that variant's `protocol_result["results"]` (`symbol`, `window`, `run_id`), never a glob of `results/` (which can hold stale retry folders). Each window's folder is found with `tools/portfolio_daily.py::find_window_equity_file` (`_pd`), and `bars.csv` is read from that folder, the sibling of its `portfolio_states.csv`. **All rows, warm-up included** (no NOT_READY drop), so the figures match the analyst's `describe` cells `abs_mean` / `share_at_cap` (PR #468, `tools/analyst_queries.py:794-798`, `FORECAST_CAP` `:134`); a blank or non-finite value is skipped in each mean, a non-numeric one makes the whole value `None`. **`None` when unreadable:** no results, a window without a `portfolio_states.csv` / `bars.csv`, an unreadable file, or a missing `forecast` / `postRebalance_current_allocation` column returns `None`; the helper catches only `(OSError, ValueError, KeyError, csv.Error)` and the grading is byte-identical whatever it returns. A variant with no `protocol_result` (not tested) carries `"forecast_shape": None` (`:16318`). **Where the key sits:** `"forecast_shape"` is a key of the per-variant dict beside `bars` (`:16354`), written to `artifacts/profit_bars_evaluation.yaml`. It is not inside `bars` or `reasons`, and `passing` / `result` are computed from `v["result"]` before and without it, so the passing rule is unchanged. Information only: nothing reads it, it is no bar and no gate. **campaign_memory does not carry it:** `tools/campaign_memory.py::profit_bars_block` (`:409`) builds each variant as `out_variants[vid] = {"result": ..., "bars": compact}` (`:457`) and returns `variants: out_variants` (`:474`), so the memory entry (`profit_bars`, `:720`) has no `forecast_shape` (the `:647` the ruling cites is the docstring of `build_memory_entry`, which calls that function). **Schema:** `workflow_artifacts/schemas/campaign_memory.schema.json` `$defs/profit_bars` (`:163`) is `additionalProperties: false` per variant with `result` and `bars` only (`:177-179`), and it validates the memory entry, not `profit_bars_evaluation.yaml`; the memory entry does not carry the key, so nothing warns and the schema is unchanged. No other schema in `workflow_artifacts/schemas/` names `profit_bars_evaluation`. **No lookahead:** reads bars the backtest already wrote, after grading inputs are fixed; the figures are an input to no bar, gate or later decision. **Tests:** `tests/test_d150_forecast_shape.py` (9 passed): hand-computed `abs_mean_forecast` 13.9, `share_at_cap` 0.4, `abs_mean_allocation` 0.65, `n_bars` 12, `n_windows` 4 over two windows x two coins (values +20, -20, 19.5, nan, a blank allocation, a NOT_READY first row); a stale extra results folder not in `protocol_result` is ignored; a missing `bars.csv`, a missing column and a non-numeric value give `None` and the evaluation equals a run where the helper is patched to return `None`, bar for bar; the key sits beside `bars` and in no bar row or reason; `result` / `passing` equal a run with the helper patched to `None`; `profit_bars_block` carries only `result` and `bars`. Every test file that references `_evaluate_profit_bars_every_backtest`, `profit_bars_evaluation` or `profit_bars_block` (16 test files, the new one included, plus `tests/fixtures/profit_bars_golden/golden_harness.py`), one process: 685 passed, 7 skipped; no existing test needed a change. The test files that read this log (`test_e061_c1_7_first_run_kit`, `test_e062_s2b3a_normalisation`, `test_e062_s2b3c_window_fingerprint`, `test_e075_pr3_trade_tests`, `test_grid_evaluation`) plus the new file: 292 passed. | E-062 (profit bars); D-047 | operator ruling, 2026-10-18 (item 7) | `strategy-research/workflow/run_phase1_research.py` (`_variant_forecast_shape`, `_evaluate_profit_bars_every_backtest`); `strategy-research/tests/test_d150_forecast_shape.py` | built (no flag) |
| D-151 | 2026-10-19 | **A claim always carries a vehicle; the analyst records what it measured but cannot yet turn into a claim as observations; profit and cost are evidence, never the claim. Declared prompt change plus a code change; no flag.** **Ruling (operator rulings, 2026-10-19, the analyst items; no verbatim quote is kept here -- the rulings are summarised, not quoted):** the empty vehicle (`vehicle: []`) is withdrawn for every claim kind; a claim carries the strategy change that follows from the observation, and the child run on the next fold is the proof, so no confirmed mechanism is needed; a behaviour with no such change is recorded under the `no_claim`, not claimed. **Declared prompt change (`workflow_artifacts/skills/analyst/SKILL.md`, `forecast.md`, `trade_efficiency.md`; `tools/analyst_session.py`, the cost-model section title):** (1) the vehicle rule: a claim carries the strategy change that follows from the observation; the child run on the next fold is the proof; no confirmed mechanism is needed; graded per D-051 (a signal parameter, a transform, a new component, or a detector condition with a graded strategy under it); never a side excluded, a clip at zero, a threshold switch, or a rule fitted to one observation; no exception sentence. (2) The empty-vehicle bullet is deleted and replaced by "no empty vehicle": if the analyst cannot say what to change it does not have a claim yet and asks the next why, or records the behaviour under `no_claim.observations`. (3) The memory-view bullet: a claim confirmed on this strategy is settled; a new claim on it names a reason it holds and the change that follows from that reason; "`vehicle: []`, kind `execution_behaviour`" is dropped. (4) Step 3 gains the thinking step (observation, then why, then the graded forecast shaped by it: which quantity, how it scales, where it is near zero) and the rule that when `windows_claimed_sign` is below `windows_with_value` the analyst runs the selector by window, then describes `past_return_<n>` or `trailing_vol_<n>` by window to name the condition and claims it through past_return or trailing_vol; a behaviour that holds under a named condition and not in its complement is a `conditional_behaviour` claim with the condition in its selector. (5) Step 5: a `conditional_effect` reply with `compiles_as_claim` true that clears the floor is written as a claim or recorded as an observation; the only `killed_by` is a query of this session that measured a rival, named; "thin", "no mechanism yet" and the expected rank are not reasons. (6) The last guard rail now reads, in its conditional form (operator, 2026-10-19), "While every bar's regime is unknown: no claim on regime or regime_at_entry; a market condition is claimed through past_return, trailing_vol or any selector in the vocabulary." (7) Profit and cost (the description line, the objective, the guard rail, `forecast.md`, `trade_efficiency.md`, the run-context cost-model title in `tools/analyst_session.py`): profit and cost are measurements of this run; cost share, fees and net are evidence, never the claim; a cost claim names the cause of the weak gross edge (the edge lasts one bar, the forecast changes sign on most bars, the forecast is large when the move is small, the signal predicts only the next bar); its test tests that cause; its vehicle changes that cause. `trade_efficiency.md` names `window_economics` as the first read; that query is a separate PR (PR-B) and is not built by this row, so until it merges the tool is refused by the session like any unknown tool. The skill's own claim example now shows a non-empty vehicle (a signal-parameter change) and the `no_claim` example shows `observations`. **The constant:** `EMPTY_VEHICLE_KINDS = ()` (`tools/reader_proposals.py:142`) applies to a MODEL's answer: `vehicle: []` is refused for every claim kind, `execution_behaviour` included, with new refusal messages in `_check_envelope` (`:374`). `claim_card.FOLDS_CLAIM_KINDS` is unchanged (the kind stays legal; a vehicle is required). **Written files (operator ruling 2026-10-19 item 3):** no silent tolerance is kept (the first draft's `WRITTEN_EMPTY_VEHICLE_KINDS` is removed). The ten proposals written with `vehicle: []` before the ruling (trade_efficiency-run_066-2, -071-4, -075-1, -078-1, -079-1, -095-1, -097-1; forecast_power-run_078-1, -094-1, -095-1; claim kind `execution_behaviour`, the only kind D-089 (a) let a model write with an empty vehicle) are deactivated, nothing rewritten: a written side finding whose `vehicle` is `[]` (any kind, `from_model` False only) passes `_check_envelope`, and `load_proposals` (`tools/reader_proposals.py:515`) flags its flattened item `legacy_no_vehicle: true` (`LEGACY_NO_VEHICLE` `:148`; on the in-memory item only, never written to a file). Decide-next drops such an item from the pool before the queue check (`tools/decide_next.py:2194`) and records it as an ineligible candidate whose only feasibility reason is `legacy_no_vehicle` (`_legacy_no_vehicle_candidate` `:1982`: INFEASIBLE, no gate evaluated, never ranked). A model's answer with `vehicle: []` stays refused. Records are unchanged: `confirmations.yaml`, `campaign_memory.yaml` and the memory view keep the four done rows as knowledge (fold_confirm's source item and campaign_memory's proposal ids read the flagged item as before). The empty-vehicle branch of `_candidate` (`empty_vehicle_needs_a_source_config`, `tools/decide_next.py:1659`) is left in place; no loaded proposal reaches it any more. **The observation record:** `no_claim.observations` is an optional list (absent = none; `[]` accepted) of mappings with exactly the keys `statement`, `test`, `evidence`, `floors_met` (`OBSERVATION_KEYS`, `tools/analyst_session.py:400`; `observation_errors` `:428`): `statement` a non-empty text; `test` the id of an ok `conditional_effect` call of the same session; `evidence` citations passing `check_citations` with at least one of `test`'s own result; `floors_met` a bool, checked against the logged result where it can tell (`floors_met_in_log` `:404`: contradicted either way is refused; `min_eras` is not in the result, so a floor on it alone is not contradicted). A recorded observation has its statement's uncited numbers masked (D-096) and listed as unverified, and code adds a `spec` (the measured test block without its result, and its `spec_hash`; `_observation_spec` `:479`). The reading of a `no_claim` with observations is identical to one without. **Where it is written:** the analyst record `artifacts/analyst/<lens>.yaml`; `tools/campaign_memory.py::analyst_observations` (`:417`) copies an accepted `no_claim` last attempt's observations (statement, test, evidence, floors_met, spec, plus the lens and a ref) into the run's `campaign_memory.yaml` entry under `observations`, set by `build_memory_entry` (`:775`) only when non-empty (absent, not `[]`, otherwise, so every other entry is unchanged); a malformed observation in an accepted record raises; `workflow_artifacts/schemas/campaign_memory.schema.json` gained the optional key. **How the memory view shows it:** `tools/analyst_memory_view.py::_memory_observations` (`:202`) adds, only when there is one, `observations_note` and `observations` to the view, one row per observation with `source_run`, `lens`, the masked `statement`, `fold_observed`, `floors_met`, `status` "observed, no vehicle yet" (`OBSERVED` `:95`), the test's mechanical spec (no result, no number) and the `source_strategy`; a view with no observation is unchanged. **Supersedes:** D-089 item (a) (a claim of kind `execution_behaviour` may change nothing, `vehicle: []`: the child re-runs the same strategy on the next fold and the strategy's own behaviour there is the test; `EMPTY_VEHICLE_KINDS` was equal to `claim_card.FOLDS_CLAIM_KINDS`) for every answer a model gives; D-089 item (b) (several coins pooled per window) is untouched. D-145 item (b) (the memory-view rule: the new claim on a settled strategy is one reason it holds, "vehicle: [], kind `execution_behaviour`", confirmed on the next fold) and item (c) (the vehicle rule: a config change only when the memory view shows its mechanism confirmed, "without a confirmed mechanism the claim is the `vehicle: []` kind") are superseded by items (3) and (1) above. D-145 item (a) (step 3: when a mechanism query fails and the behaviour holds, name the next mechanism) and item (d) (the "edge that lasts one bar" worked example: two tests on `forecast <= -10`, horizon 1 direction `less` and horizons 2-3 direction `greater`) stand; the example's closing sentence now names a signal-parameter vehicle instead of `vehicle: []`, its tests are unchanged. **No lookahead:** a prompt change and a record of what the session already measured on this run's own bars and trades; an observation is a citation of a logged query of the same session, nothing new is read. **Known gap (awaits the operator's vehicle-format decision):** the vehicle rule names "a transform, a new component", but the vehicle item `{component_id, field, before, after}` resolved by `decide_next.resolve_patch` (`tools/decide_next.py:551-576`) can only change the value of an existing field, so a vehicle that adds a transform or a component cannot be expressed yet and is refused. **Operator decisions after review (2026-10-19):** (a) a `no_claim` carries at most 3 observations, each citing a different test (refused in `observation_errors` otherwise; the same query, or the same test block run again, counts as the same test), because the prompt grows with every recorded observation; the SKILL.md `no_claim` paragraph says so. (b) The memory view keeps the newest 20 observation rows (`OBSERVATION_ROWS_MAX`), the same mechanism as `TRIED_IDEAS_MAX_ROWS`: newest by the source run's `recorded_at`, then run order; the view states `observations_max_rows`, `observations_in_memory` and `omitted_older_observations`. (c) An observation row's test shows its `floor` (counts of events, windows, blocks or eras only; claims' test rows are unchanged), because `floors_met` means nothing without it. (d) `_observation_spec` and the view deep-copy the test slots so two observations never write YAML aliases. (e) `trade_efficiency.md` says the pipeline rule itself is not a vehicle; the vehicle changes the forecast the rule acts on. (f) The vehicle rule wording is unchanged. (g) Volatility claims are ON (operator ruling 2026-10-19 item 2): a market condition is claimed through past_return or trailing_vol (the last guard rail, item 6 above, and step 3's by-window text carry this wording). `trailing_vol` is ungated in `claim_tests` by PR-A2 (a separate PR, merged after this one and before the smoke); this PR changes no code. Step 3's by-window step (item C, option (a)) runs the selector by window, then describes `past_return_<n>` or `trailing_vol_<n>` by window (`analyst_queries.describe`, `by="window"`, free of comparisons) to name the condition, then claims it through past_return or trailing_vol. PR-C later changes the last clause to all_of. **Tests:** `tests/test_pr_a_vehicle_observations.py` (new), `tests/test_d089_vehicle_and_coins.py` and `tests/test_e075_pr5_analyst.py` (the assertions that pinned `vehicle: []` changed with it; the written-file flag and decide-next's `legacy_no_vehicle` record in `tests/test_pr_a_vehicle_observations.py` and `tests/test_d089_vehicle_and_coins.py`), results in the PR. | E-075 (analyst); D-051, D-089, D-096, D-145 | operator rulings, 2026-10-19 (item 3: written empty vehicles) | `workflow_artifacts/skills/analyst/SKILL.md`, `forecast.md`, `trade_efficiency.md`; `tools/analyst_session.py`; `tools/reader_proposals.py`; `tools/decide_next.py`; `tools/campaign_memory.py`; `tools/analyst_memory_view.py`; `workflow_artifacts/schemas/campaign_memory.schema.json`; `tests/test_pr_a_vehicle_observations.py`; `tests/test_d089_vehicle_and_coins.py` | built (no flag; declared prompt change; written empty-vehicle items load flagged `legacy_no_vehicle` and are ineligible, operator ruling 2026-10-19 item 3) |
| D-152 | 2026-10-19 | **`signed_power`: the graded soft dead-zone op. No flag; byte-identical for every config that does not use it.** **What it computes (`trading-bot/strategies/registry.py:207-217`, `_signed_power`):** `pivot * sign(v) * (|v| / pivot) ** exponent` on the running value. With `exponent` 2 and the default pivot 10, 5 gives 2.5, 10 stays 10 and 15 gives 22.5 (negative inputs mirror, a zero stays 0); `exponent` 1 is the identity. A value below the pivot in absolute size is damped towards 0 smoothly, with no jump, which is the graded dead zone that D-051 (amended 2026-09-30) left as ticket CUL-354 when it banned the `threshold_filter` / `volume_filter` cliff in `strategies`. **Params and bounds (operator ruling 2026-10-19; `SIGNED_POWER_DEFAULTS` `:181`, `SIGNED_POWER_BOUNDS` `:182`, both inclusive):** `exponent` is required, a finite number with 1 <= exponent <= 3, with no default (a default of 1 would make an op configured without one a silent no-op; 1 is the identity, above 3 over-amplifies); `pivot` is optional, default 10 (the forecast scale's average absolute value), a finite number with 10 <= pivot <= 20 (the standardise target to its cap: a pivot below the typical value amplifies the typical forecast, which is a re-scaling, not a dead zone). Bools, strings, None, inf, NaN, an exponent outside 1 to 3, a pivot outside 10 to 20 and non-dict params are refused by `signed_power_param_errors` (`:185-204`); the op raises `ValueError` on exactly those params (`:207-211`). **Worst case: 20 becomes 80** (pivot 10, exponent 3, on `standardise`'s cap of 20); the engine's +/-20 clip is on the combined forecast, not on each component, so it does not bound the op and one component can carry up to 80 into the combination. **Non-finite refused, as `vol_target` does:** a non-finite input (NaN, +/-inf) or a non-finite output (an unscaled raw value so large that the power overflows) raises `ValueError` (`:211-216`). **Where a refused value lands, which is why placement is ruled (operator ruling 2026-10-19, placement option 1):** in a strategies component's `transforms` it raises inside `generate_forecast`, which `generate_signals` catches (`trading-bot/strategies/strategy_base.py:398-405`), so that bar's output is forecast 0.0, regime `ERROR`, with the error logged; the bot then rebalances toward 0 through the normal path (this is not a hold; the same component error path as `vol_target`). In `history_transforms` the raise happens in `update()`: `AdvancedStrategy.update` catches it (`main_strategy.py:174`), but it aborts the rest of `strategy_engine.update` for that bar, so the components after the failing one miss their history update and the histories fall out of step; in the regime detector it escapes `generate_signals` and only the per-candle catch in the bot remains (no trade, no snapshot). **A NaN can reach the op** (a ready component can emit NaN, and `standardise` passes NaN through): before the bounds a NaN propagated and the CUL-274 guard held the allocation; now it raises here, which is a flatten attempt (forecast 0.0, rebalance toward 0 through the normal path), not a hold. **Scalar, not a scale op:** it is not in `HISTORY_OPS`, `TRANSFORM_MIN_PERIODS["signed_power"]` is 1 (`:319`), and it reads only the running value, never the history or the bar data (**why this cannot leak**). It does not put a component on the forecast scale, so D-149 still needs `standardise` (or `vol_target`, or a blocks regime with `block_standardisation`) in the same `transforms`: `tools/forecast_rules.py` `_not_standardised` (`:117`) passes `[standardise, signed_power]` and refuses `[signed_power]` alone. It places after `standardise`; before it, V5 refuses the history op that follows. **Allowed in `strategies` as far as D-051 goes (placement is a separate rule, V16 below):** D-051's amendment bans only `threshold_filter` and `volume_filter` (`DEAD_ZONE_OPS`, `forecast_rules.py:49`), because the position jumps from 0 to the threshold; `signed_power` has no jump (the output is continuous in `v`), so `[standardise, signed_power]` passes D-051 and D-149 (tested). **Validator:** V16 (`trading-bot/tools/validate_config.py:404-451`, right after V15) checks every `signed_power` step in every transforms / history_transforms list that `_all_transform_lists` yields and, in V16 only, in the same lists of each block's source detector (`strategies.regimes.<r>.blocks[i].source.regime_detector`, which `strategy_engine._Block` runs with its own `ConfigDrivenRegimeEngine`: its `vetoes[*].transforms` and its `regimes.<r>.components[*].transforms` / `history_transforms`; `_all_transform_lists` itself is unchanged, so every other rule's output is too), with `signed_power_param_errors`, the function the op uses, so V16 fails on exactly the params the op raises on, with V15's message format (`VIOLATION V16 <loc>[k].params: <msg>`), and is silent for every config without the op. **Placement rule (V16, the V14 pattern; operator ruling 2026-10-19, placement option 1):** `signed_power` is allowed only in a strategies component's `transforms`, in a regime without `blocks`; anywhere else (a regime-detector component's `transforms` or `history_transforms`, `vetoes[*].transforms`, a strategies `history_transforms`, a strategies regime with `blocks`, any list of a block's source detector) V16 emits one line `VIOLATION V16 <loc>[k]: signed_power is allowed only in a strategies component's `transforms`, in a regime without `blocks` (a value it refuses raises there, inside generate_signals' guard)`, and its params are still checked there as well. The test is the shared helper `_in_strategy_transforms` (`validate_config.py:356-361`), which V14 uses too; V14 still has the same block-source-detector gap for `vol_target` (it walks only `_all_transform_lists`; pre-existing, not changed here). No rule on the position within the list (`[standardise, signed_power, clip]` is fine; before `standardise` it is V5's matter). **Bound on the output:** after `standardise` (|v| <= its cap, 20) the output is finite and at most `pivot * (20 / pivot) ** exponent`, which over the allowed grid peaks at 80 (pivot 10, exponent 3; tested over the whole grid). **Byte identity:** no existing op's code or min-periods entry changed; a config without `signed_power` runs exactly as before, V16 adds nothing for it, and `validate()` on the standardise-only fixtures of `test_standardise_op.py` is still `[]`. **Docs and name lists (declared prompt change; the config-writing stage reads both files):** `strategy-research/docs/STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md` gets the `signed_power` row beside `threshold_filter` ("the graded dead zone; allowed only in a strategies component's `transforms`, in a regime without `blocks`"), the V16 row, "Eighteen ops", and the sentence "A graded dead zone that starts from 0 does not exist yet." now points at the op; `COMPONENT_CATALOG.md` says "the only eighteen", lists `signed_power` among the scalar ops, and has a variant-pattern bullet ("Soften a dead zone"); `trading-bot/DOC/USER_GUIDE.md` lists `signed_power` among the scalar ops and counts 18 ops in `TRANSFORM_OPS_REGISTRY`; `workflow/run_phase1_research.py` `_TRANSFORM_OP_NAMES` gains `signed_power` (O-21: a component_gap's `tried` must name a transform). **Tests:** `trading-bot/tests/test_signed_power_op.py` (131: exponent 1 is the identity, the 5 / 10 / 15 values at exponent 2, sign, zero, a non-default pivot (20), the inclusive bounds 1 / 3 / 10 / 20 accepted and 0.999 / 3.001 / 9.999 / 20.001 refused with the new messages, the 20 to 80 worst case and the whole bounded grid staying finite within +/-80, NaN / +/-inf input and an overflowing output raising, every param refusal and the op raising the same, V16 lines equal to the param check's including bound violations, V16 placement (operator ruling 2026-10-19: allowed in a strategies component's `transforms` in a regime without `blocks`, with `standardise`, `signed_power`, `clip` giving no V16 line; exactly one placement line for a strategies `history_transforms`, a strategies regime with `blocks`, a regime-detector component's `transforms` and `history_transforms`, and `vetoes[*].transforms`; the former "no placement rule" test flipped; in a block's source detector, exactly one placement line for its `vetoes[*].transforms` and for its regime component's `transforms` and `history_transforms`, params still checked there, only V16 lines added, a well-formed block fixture without the op validating `[]`, and a malformed combiner never raising), V16 silent without the op, scalar and min-periods 1, the `standardise` then `signed_power` pipeline, other scalar ops unchanged); `strategy-research/tests/test_d051_d053_forecast_rules.py` (`[standardise, signed_power]` passes D-051 and D-149; `[signed_power]` alone is refused by D-149). Three existing op-set pins updated for the new key (the `standardise` PR's precedent; each still pins that every other op is unchanged): `test_standardise_op.py::test_every_other_op_and_min_periods_entry_is_unchanged`, `test_vol_target_transform.py::test_every_other_op_is_unchanged`, `test_e060_block_standardisation.py::test_transform_ops_registry_is_unchanged`. `test_design_guide_in_sync.py` (the guide and the catalogue state the registry's op count and list) passes with the doc edits. trading-bot, 6 files (the new one, `test_standardise_op.py`, `test_vol_target_transform.py`, `test_e060_block_standardisation.py`, `test_validate_config.py`, `test_history_op_readiness.py`), one process: 327 passed; strategy-research, 5 files (`test_d051_d053_forecast_rules.py`, `test_design_guide_in_sync.py`, `test_design_guide_examples.py`, `test_e061_c1_7_first_run_kit.py`, `test_o21_component_gap_tried.py`), one process: 155 passed (the `test_d051_d053_forecast_rules.py` example config uses an in-bounds pivot, 15). Bounds and non-finite refusal added by operator ruling 2026-10-19; V16 placement added by operator ruling 2026-10-19 (placement option 1). Closes CUL-354. Not handled (as for `standardise`, D-148): an int param too large for a float (e.g. exponent `10**400`) makes `signed_power_param_errors` raise TypeError from `np.isfinite` instead of returning an error; JSON configs do not produce such values; code not changed. | CUL-354; D-051; D-149 | advisory session ruling, 2026-10-19 (PR-D); number D-152 fixed by the operator | `trading-bot/strategies/registry.py` (`_signed_power`, `signed_power_param_errors`, `TRANSFORM_MIN_PERIODS`); `trading-bot/tools/validate_config.py` (V16); `strategy-research/docs/STRATEGY_DESIGN_GUIDE.md`; `strategy-research/docs/COMPONENT_CATALOG.md`; `trading-bot/DOC/USER_GUIDE.md`; `strategy-research/workflow/run_phase1_research.py` (`_TRANSFORM_OP_NAMES`); `trading-bot/tests/test_signed_power_op.py`; `strategy-research/tests/test_d051_d053_forecast_rules.py`; three op-set pin tests | built (no flag; declared prompt change) |
